- **electrobun-align**: Alignment wizard for existing Electrobun projects. Safe to run at any time — never modifies without permission. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-align.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-build**: Guided Electrobun build command — selects environment, verifies signing credentials, runs the build, and summarizes artifacts produced. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-build.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-feature**: Build a complete Electrobun feature end-to-end using the two-agent team — UI agent designs views and produces the RPC contract, backend agent implements bun-side wiring. Produces all renderer files plus updated bun entrypoint and electrobun.config.ts. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-feature.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-init**: Scaffold a new Electrobun desktop app project from scratch. Creates full directory structure, package.json, electrobun.config.ts, tsconfig.json, and entry point files. - **electrobun-menu**: Generate an ApplicationMenu with File/Edit/View/Help items wired to application-menu-clicked events, plus optional Tray setup. Asks whether to forward events to a webview via RPC. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-menu.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-release**: Guided step-by-step release workflow for Electrobun apps — version bump, build, artifact verification, upload to CDN, and auto-updater verification. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-release.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-rpc**: Introspect existing Electrobun bun-process and renderer files to generate a complete type-safe RPC schema. Reads your code, infers all cross-process calls, and writes the defineElectrobunRPC + Electroview.defineRPC boilerplate. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-rpc.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-sdlc**: Run the full 8-stage Electrobun SDLC pipeline for a feature — researcher, architect, planner, dev squad, QA engineer, test writer, alignment agent, docs agent. Produces tested, documented, complete features. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-sdlc.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-setup**: Run immediately after electrobun init. Derives the project name from electrobun.config.ts (never assumes a hardcoded name), scans for existing .github infrastructure, then walks through a wizard to create CI/CD, release pipeline, test scaffold, docs, CLAUDE.md, AI code review, trust scoring, and pre-push hooks for a production-ready project. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-setup.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-test**: Run Electrobun Kitchen Sink tests, write new test coverage, or generate the feature manifest. Works with both automated and interactive test suites. - **electrobun-wgpu**: Set up a WebGPU rendering window in an Electrobun project. Creates a GpuWindow with WGPUView, a minimal WGSL pass-through shader, render loop, and KEEPALIVE array. - **electrobun-window**: Generate a new BrowserWindow with a matching renderer entry point and type-safe RPC schema connecting them. - **electrobun-workflow**: Show the full Electrobun pipeline status for the current project and jump-start any stage. Detects init/dev/build/release state automatically. - **element.list-by-classification**: List every element on the plan that is classified under the given ClassificationItem in the given ClassificationSystem. The audit primitive for "show me every IfcWall" or "every fire-rated assembly". ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/archicad-29/commands/element.list-by-classification.md) · ★7)
- **element.list-by-trade**: List elements in the active Allplan document filtered by **trade** (architecture / structure / MEP / civil) and, optionally, by a regex matched against the element's **layer name**. Returns `id`, `type`, `layer`, and `trade` per element — the audit primitive for "show me every structural element" or "every MEP object on a `*-DRAFT` layer". ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/allplan-2025/commands/element.list-by-trade.md) · ★7)
- **element.set-layer-by-pattern**: Move every element whose current layer name matches a regex onto a target layer. Used for cleaning imported models or normalizing DWG-derived geometry. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/archicad-29/commands/element.set-layer-by-pattern.md) · ★7)
- **elements**: Each `## id` section adds its paragraphs to that command as notes, a `See also:` line as related commands and each `### title` with its `lua` block as an example, which runs in the tests and must end in `assert`s. A section naming several commands goes to each of them, before their own. The command reference in `docs/SCRIPTING.md` is generated from them. ([gilbertorconde/printCAD](https://github.com/gilbertorconde/printCAD/blob/HEAD/crates/workbenches/wb_inspect/src/commands/elements.md) · ★7)

([BlackPool25/Elenchus-Crucible](https://github.com/BlackPool25/Elenchus-Crucible/blob/HEAD/packages/elenchus-crucible/commands/elenchus.md) · ★1)

Controls sentinel_paused file (NOT sentinel_enabled). ([EmpiricaAI/empirica](https://github.com/EmpiricaAI/empirica/blob/HEAD/empirica/plugins/claude-code-integration/commands/empirica.md) · ★258)

- **empirical-prompt-tuning**: agent 向けテキスト指示（skill / slash command / task プロンプト / CLAUDE.md 節 / コード生成プロンプト）を、バイアスを排した実行者に動かしてもらい、両面（実行者の自己申告 + 指示側メトリクス）で評価して反復改善する手法。改善が頭打ちになるまで回す。プロンプトや skill を新規作成・大幅改訂した直後、またはエージェントの挙動が期待通りにならない原因を指示側の曖昧さに求めたいときに使う。 ([Yu-Muro/BasicKnowledgeForWeb](https://github.com/Yu-Muro/BasicKnowledgeForWeb/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/empirical-prompt-tuning.md))

- **employer-overview**: Jobiba employer hiring overview ([Bhagsa123/jobiba-mcp](https://github.com/Bhagsa123/jobiba-mcp/blob/HEAD/commands/employer-overview.md))
- **employment-cold-start-interview**: 冷启动设置——从你的员工手册/规章制度和解除备忘录了解你的省市司法管辖区足迹和升级规则。询问哪些省市和国家有员工，读取种子文档，构建一个了解哪些规则在哪里适用的升级表。在全新安装时、当 PLUGIN.md 仍有 [PLACEHOLDER] 标记时，或当使用 --redo 或 --check-integrations 重新运行时使用。 ([JsonShare/PrismClaw](https://github.com/JsonShare/PrismClaw/blob/HEAD/backend/.prismclaw/plugins/legal-chineselaw/employment-legal/commands/cold-start-interview.md) · ★2)
- **employment-customize**: 引导式自定义你的劳动法执业档案——更改一项内容而无需重新运行整个冷启动访谈。调整省市足迹、风险姿态、升级联系人、招聘合规规则、解除审查规则、员工手册立场、调查偏好或事项工作空间路径。当用户说"更改我的[某项]"、"添加省市"、"更新我的档案"、"编辑我的配置"或"自定义"时使用。 ([JsonShare/PrismClaw](https://github.com/JsonShare/PrismClaw/blob/HEAD/backend/.prismclaw/plugins/legal-chineselaw/employment-legal/commands/customize.md) · ★2)
- **employment-handbook**: Create or review employee handbook policies covering key employment topics ([sponticelli/business-claude-plugins](https://github.com/sponticelli/business-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/legal/legal-compliance/commands/employment-handbook.md) · ★1)
- **employment-matter-workspace**: 管理事项工作空间——新建、列表、切换、关闭或分离（执业级）。创建、列出、切换、关闭和分离活跃事项，使一个客户参与的上下文永不泄漏到另一个。当多客户从业者说"新事项"、"切换事项"、"列出我的事项"、"关闭此事项"或需要管理哪个事项活跃时使用。 ([JsonShare/PrismClaw](https://github.com/JsonShare/PrismClaw/blob/HEAD/backend/.prismclaw/plugins/legal-chineselaw/employment-legal/commands/matter-workspace.md) · ★2)
